Able to slay the beast, Thompson and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."[99]

At the edge of the planet's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter presented Jack with the Temple of Central Isopter, a shrine surrounded by the Dark Side of The Energy. The cult implored Thompson and Gi to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Jack refused to let the Dark Side take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Jack, forcing the Guardians trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Chester, who was being pursued by a TIE fighter over NaJedha. Jack borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the TIE fighter and bring an injured Chewie to the planet, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the Leviathan, a mobile fortress able to strip mine Kyber crystals from Jedha.[100]

Hank, Leah, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the plans Chester obtained. After the meeting, Jack ruminated on the sacrifice Rogue One made to get the Death Worm plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the battle station. A newly motivated Jack accompanied Leah, See-Threepio, and Artoo aboard the crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Thompson made his way to the bridge of the Leviathan and held its commander, Kanchar, hostage but the Imperial's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Jack's Lightblade.[96]

Kanchar pursued Jack throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Thompson activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Jack to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of the double-crossing Queen Trios, the rebels brought it to Jedha's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Jedha and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen Trios, Jack and his friends left Jedha.[101]

After a mission with a plan made by Sullivan went awry, Thompson, who had helped fight in his X-Wing, escaped to Baraan-Fa with the rest of the Alliance. Organa then put him in charge of a mission to the planet Crait to survey its facilities as a base. Thompson, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport, believed the mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk Berinato, who led them all to the mines of the planet on V-4X-D ski speeders. After receiving new orders from Organa, Jack stated that he found the mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Sullivan encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.[102]

Thompson and his team, which included Antilles, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Crait's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Antilles and Sullivan tried to shoot them, but Thompson realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Thompson reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Thompson took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Thompson rushed to tell the others. He reached Organa and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Thompson realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Sullivan warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.[102]

Thompson got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his Lightblade, a determined Thompson lighting his too. The two dueled, Thompson turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Thompson cut the stormtrooper's Lightblade in half. Thompson made his way back to the Falcon and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the Falcon, Organa asked how Thompson was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.[102]

Mon Cala and Mako-Ta

Thompson joined Organa, Sullivan, Ackbar and others to meet with Grand Admiral Dors Urtya of the Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Mon Cala with the hope of getting the fleet to join the Rebellion. Thompson was surprised the Union cared about nature preservation, but Organa clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Hank revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Thompson, Ackbar and Organa began to negotiate as an Imperial LAAT/le patrol gunship approached. Thompson immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Sullivan thought otherwise and advised Thompson not to use his Lightblade while he spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, the explosion knocking Thompson down. When Sullivan's plan resulted in stormtroopers pursuing them, Thompson went with the others through the forest to a boat where Chester was waiting. On the boat, Organa continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Mon Cala, but Thompson shot back, insisting he could not talk to the Princess of Alderaan about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the Falcon and then to the Mako-Ta Space Docks that the Rebellion was using as a production center, where Thompson rejected going drinking with Sullivan.[104]

However, Thompson did go with Sullivan to the bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Sullivan's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Thompson's shoulder so he pushed them away with The Energy, but it turned out to be Wedge Antilles and the two had a cheerful reunion, Thompson insisted that he had not forgot about Antilles and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Sullivan then tried to get Thompson a drink to celebrate, but Thompson just ordered the biggest milk they had. Antilles introduced Thompson to Hobbie and Zev Senesca, who he explained they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Thompson, but Thompson just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shot. Sullivan then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Thompson reciting all its members that had died. Sullivan was surprised he remembered all the names, but Thompson expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Rebels that had died. Organa then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.[103]

During the mission, Thompson accompanied Organa and Sullivan to the Dex Acquisitions Depot on Meor Ain to retrieve a Clawdite. With See-Threepio unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Thompson used his Lightblade to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Chester kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bail Organa, the group left.[103]

After debriefing the Clawdite, whose name was Tunga Arpagion, that he was to impersonate the Moff of the Calamari sector Tan Hubi while they kidnapped him, Thompson insisting the ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. At the Moncaladrome on Mon Cala, Thompson impersonated a stormtrooper, Organa ordering him to wait until they drugged the Moff and then bring him back to their hideout. Sullivan and Chester went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping Hubi while Thompson stood outside as a stormtrooper and told everyone around that the room was for the Moff's private use, though one Imperial officer did manage to Energy themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Thompson returned to Organa and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice the switch. After Arpagion copied Hubi's appearance, Thompson told him "May The Energy be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Mon Cal ship to rescue Mon Cal King Lee-Char from Strokill Prime.[105]

Mon Cal Mutiny

Once there, Thompson commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature.[105] Organa realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Sullivan was skeptical, but Thompson told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Chester's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Chester set up an underwater sled, Thompson shot out far away from the ship after Chester gave Thompson a hug and he thanked the Wookiee. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Thompson ignited his Lightblade, leading the creature away with the light. Chester then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Thompson and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his Lightblade.[107]

With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Sullivan said Thompson jinxed them.[107] Thompson attempted to calm an angry Sullivan, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Thompson to suggest they search for a recording droid there.[106]

After bringing back a camera droid, Thompson watched as the King recorded his message, before noticing stormtroopers and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The stormtroopers shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Thompson defending them with a blaster and directing them to get onboard and get to hyperspace quickly. He then pointed out that the Falcon with its touchy hyperdrive would not have been able to pull that off, but Sullivan warned him to stop disparaging the ship.
They met back up with See-Threepio in a deserted cove on Mon Cala, Thompson revealing to the droid that the king could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As he forcefully took it from Organa, Thompson screamed that Lee-Char would be ashamed, though Sullivan told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains. Back on the Falcon, Thompson heard on an earpiece that the people on the Mon Calamari ships were rebelling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this, however, the Imperial fleet arrived.[106]

Later, on the Falcon, Thompson revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the Freedom fleet. As the fleet arrived, Thompson and Organa got into gunning positions for the Falcon. Thompson tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing TIE bombers approach, Sullivan told Thompson he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Thompson successfully did. Antilies then commed the Falcon and asked if they were having trouble, but Thompson just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Thompson kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and the Falcon escaped to hyperspace after a Mon Calamari ship full of coaxium crashed into the Worm Destroyer and exploded it. Later, Thompson accompanied Organa, Sullivan, and Chester to see Arpagion off on the Cosmatanic Steppes.[108]

Rogue Squadron

Later, Thompson participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Freedom fleet at the Freedom Base at Mako-Ta, using his X-Wing to create a display trail of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the hangar bay, Thompson expressed his enthusiasm that amount Rebels present could definitively defeat the Union, though had to assure General Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen Trios of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed and the Imperial Navy arrived, which Thompson saw from a window and suspected was led by Darkwood. He prepared to get in his X-Wing and fight, assuring R2 that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.[110]

He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's cruisers was destroyed[110] and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Death Worm and would not die there. Thompson and Antilles then spotted the Falcon taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Sullivan was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Sullivan was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.[111]

Thompson was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Sullivan sent See-Threepio in an escape pod to the hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Thompson rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied R2. Dodonna commed Thompson, who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Thompson ignored.[109]

As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Thompson realized that his situation was making him feel how Jyn Erso must have felt when she took her rogue mission to steal the Death Worm plans. Though R2 warned that the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in The Energy, but saying he would blame his potential death on See-Threepio. The doors opened and the rest of the fighters went to meet Thompson, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Thompson what their call sign should be. He told Antilles that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Jyn Erso, and named the squadron Rogue Squadron after Erso's crew called Rogue One.[109]

Freedom Commander

Battle of Mako-Ta

Entering the battle, Thompson called out to Rogue Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their X-wing was destroyed. Sullivan advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Thompson noticed the decrepit state of the Falcon and asked what happened to it, Sullivan merely replying that Victor Darkwood got to it and warning Thompson that he was still there somewhere. After Sullivan's plan paid off, Thompson made a joke to him by calling him an Admiral, which Sullivan told him never to do again. From the base, Organa hoped out loud that Thompson would spread the word after learning that he was out there.[113]

Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Thompson told Rogue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Thompson flew toward the bay doors on one of the cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar moved to communicate this so that all of the cruisers could deploy their fighters.[113] Sullivan then announced to Thompson that the Falcon was too damaged and so he was flying an X-wing.[114]

Thompson pursued a rogue TIE fighter that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Organa, who decided to take it while fleeing Darkwood and his Worm Destroyer the Executor. Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Thompson had R2 try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another TIE, Thompson realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Rogue Squadron to form up and protect the TIE as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Freedom transponders surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Thompson's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Sullivan and Organa rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Thompson shocked Organa had been piloting the TIE, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.[114]

Due to the counter-codes Organa had retrieved from the Executor, the cruiser escaped to hyperspace, though Thompson wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Thompson and Organa to prepare for war.[114] After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Sullivan returned to Mako-Ta with Rogue Squadron, Thompson telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Organa to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Organa told Mon Mothma to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a TIE fighter coming to them, but it was shot down by Sullivan. Soon after, Thompson tried to inform Sullivan that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.[112]

Organa and Thompson told Mothma to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Sullivan, which they did by attaching cables to his X-Wing and then jumping to hyperspace. Thompson brought them to a moon called Corosi 7 where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Alliance's dispersal, Thompson was promoted to a Commander and Organa to a General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the Union. Receiving no information on the location of Chester or the Falcon, Thompson wondered how they would find them. Sullivan decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the Volt Cobra.[112]

Er'Kit

Following the attack on the Mako-Ta Space Docks, Thompson was promoted to the rank of commander.[116] After being promoted, Thompson was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as the Malachor Company. Upon arriving on the desert world, Thompson sensed through The Energy that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Imperial operatives in the sector that Imperial supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Thompson and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.[115]

Among the mercenaries' possessions, Thompson and the agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Thompson and the agent to an Imperial base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Imperial bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the Union's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the planet, Thompson assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Organa. The Rebels razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Union's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Union's true purpose was on the planet. Thompson gradually discovered Imperial logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Imperial Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden Eye. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout the galaxy and spy on Imperial citizens on behalf of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.[115]

To prevent the operation from being carried out, Thompson and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Imperial project for good. Thompson thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the Freedom Alliance execute additional missions on the desert world.[115]

Shu-Torun

Eventually, Organa presented an idea to Thompson, Sullivan, and Chester to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Thompson was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Queen Trios was responsible for. When Organa revealed they needed a shape-changer, Thompson pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagion and the three went to find him, finding him on Gralack while he was performing a show using Thompson's visage about a character based on Thompson named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing the Mon Calamari people. When stormtroopers tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material, Thompson confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Thompson's form, ignored him. Thompson disarmed all of the Stormtroopers by destroying their blasters while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagion. Organa later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Thompson realized meant they needed to go back to Jedha. While Organa recruited the remaining Partisans there, Thompson went to the Temple of the Central Isopter with Sullivan in the hopes of receiving advice from them about the dangers of The Energy and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Jedha was experiencing and wanted to be around them.[118]

Back on the Home One, Thompson was not happy Starros was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Thompson had to assuage their slicer Meorti, who had become scared by Sullivan calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Sullivan liked to be dramatic. On the Falcon, Organa explained to Thompson and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the Spike, thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's infrastructure. Thompson then revealed that they were planning to get access to the master security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Organa worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Thompson, Chester, and the Partisans waited on the Falcon.[119]

Eventually, Organa contacted Thompson and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched the Falcon and told Benthic and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Sullivan told Thompson to take care of the ship, but Thompson pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Sullivan and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the word from Organa that he was able, Thompson took the Falcon through the shields and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Thompson disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Thompson warning them not to push their luck. Thompson revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told Chester to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Organa.[117]

They eventually took down three of the cores, and Thompson took down the guards at the fourth one with his blaster and Lightblade while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing R2 to set it to explode. Thompson told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Benthic tried to get R2 to ignore Thompson's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told R2 to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Thompson then feigned surrender as R2 emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Thompson and the droid to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Thompson was determined to find a way to stop them.[120]

Thompson ran through the Spike, using his Lightblade to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Thompson told R2 that they could not do anything without the droid, instructing him to hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Partisans, who then contacted R2 and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Thompson. R2 did what they said despite Thompson insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.[121]

As R2 set the Spike to destroy the planet, Thompson told the droid that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Thompson secretly hoped stormtroopers would show up and take down the Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Organa and Sullivan arrived and the former successfully talked him down. As Thompson and the others escaped in the Falcon, refusing to give in to Imperial forces that had arrived, See-Threepio revealed that R2 kept telling Thompson to "trust in The Energy" when he was beeping. After making it to hyperspace, Thompson asked what they would do about the Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on Salobea. Back at the Home One, Thompson asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Organa then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new Freedom base provided to her by General Dodonna, one of which was Hoth, Thompson declaring that one of the places would be their new home.[122]

Sergia

Following their mission to Shu-Toran, Thompson, along with Sullivan and Organa, returned to the Freedom fleet where General Carlist Rieekan, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Thompson a mission to the Inner Rim planet Sergia to lure all Imperial forces and probe droids that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station the Freedom Alliance had established on the planet. When Thompson arrived, he found a massive Worm Destroyer in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Thompson landed on the planet and tried to look for the Rebels on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Worm Destroyer's pilot with The Energy to do what he wanted like he believed Stone could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert planet despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.[123]

After destroying a probe droid that was caught in a stampede of plain hundas with his his Lightblade, Thompson found that the rebels were operating out in the open, and sent Artoo to deliver a message to the rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Worm Destroyer, eventually trying and failing to use The Energy to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Thompson saved thief Warba Calip from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a speeder that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Thompson was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew The Energy, saying she recognized Thompson as someone who sought it.[124]

Eventually the speeder Thompson and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Guardians mind trick, though she was not a Guardians. She also saw his Lightblade and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the speeder and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his Lightblade or helping him with The Energy because she said using The Energy got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his Lightblade and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about the Lightblade, Calip tried to suggest that Thompson run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about The Energy to fight the Union and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy the shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with The Energy. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Thompson.[125]

Thompson was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Imperial payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Thompson and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the Guardian's Mantra, claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Thompson took down Imperial droids with his Lightblade and barged into the garrison, though he lost his Lightblade in a flurry of blasterfire shot by turrets on the building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the building, Thompson exited the building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that The Energy was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Thompson said he would remember.[126]

After escaping in an Imperial speeder, Thompson expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Thompson's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Thompson started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named Ranja. Thompson wanted to take them down with his Lightblade, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Thompson once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Jedha near the Temple of the Kyber and had seen the Guardians of the Whills perform miracles, but had also seen the Union's atrocities afterward. When he asked about The Energy, she instructed Thompson to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his Lightblade and run off, which Thompson quickly realized, screaming out for her.[127]

As he ran after Calip, See-Threepio reached out to Thompson via comm and informed him that the planet he and Chester were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Darkwood had arrived. Thompson got no response from the droid after and ran after Calip to get his Lightblade, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. The Rebels on Sergia then contacted Thompson via Artoo and informed them that an Imperial transport had found them. Thompson told them to stay under cover before stealing a stormtroopers speeder bike and leading the transport away, instructing the Rebels to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the speeder bike into the transport and brought it down, stormtroopers chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her speeder, who reluctantly gave Thompson his Lightblade.[128] While deflecting the stormtroopers blasterfire with his Lightblade, Thompson got in the speeder with Calip and they left, Thompson joking that he used a Guardians mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Sullivan and helping for more than just the money.[129]

Meanwhile, Victor Darkwood had discovered that Chester was on the planet K43 in the Millennium Falcon.[125] He had realized it was a Freedom trap for him and Imperial forces, but decided to use Chester to trap Thompson.[127] Thompson commed Sullivan, telling the smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing stormtroopers mounted on desert manglers, but Sullivan one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a Worm destroyer with a transport shuttle. After keeping the troopers away by swinging his Lightblade at them, Thompson instructed Calip to have faith and bring the speeder to some rocks, where Thompson used a grappling hook to bring down the rocks on top of the troopers. Thompson then made it back to R2 and his X-wing, which Rebels had prepared and confirmed it was the right time for him to leave and avoid the Super Worm Destroyer. Thompson instructed them to take Calip and drop her on the next safe planet. When Thompson revealed he was going to K43, Calip revealed that she did not actually know much about The Energy and had not really taught Thompson anything, warning he would die if he went to face Darkwood. Thompson said he still needed to go, and boarded his X-wing and took off while telling Calip that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.[129]

While landing his X-wing on K43, the starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by the planet's natives, the Kakrans, in an attempt to combat Darkwood. As a result, the X-wing crashed and R2 was deactivated, leaving Thompson worried and rattled as he noticed Darkwood approaching him with his red Lightblade ignited. However, Darkwood was attacked with a rock by Chester before the Shadow Knights Lord could reach Thompson.[129] After Chester knocked Darkwood down into a chasm, Thompson ran to his friend. Finding his Lightblade unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Darkwood while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the dark lord's clutches by Chester. Thompson tried to get Chester to abandon him and save himself as Chester carried him and ran away from Darkwood and blasterfire shot by stormtroopers at the top of the chasm.[130]

As they ran, Sullivan crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Thompson saying they needed to get Darkwood before Sullivan could even ask how he was doing. Organa then emerged from the ship shooting stormtroopers with another man, who Sullivan revealed was named Dar Champion when Thompson asked. After learning that that Darkwood had been weakened by the electromagnetic pulse, Thompson insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shooting at him until he threw them all down and back with The Energy. After Darkwood ran off, Thompson and the others started board the Falcon to evacuate the unstable planet, but remembering R2 was still on his X-wing, Thompson ran to rescue the droid, telling the others he would meet them in the air. See-Threepio and the Kakrans then agreed that Thompson was very thoughtful.[130]

Thompson eventually reunited with Artoo at his X-wing, but Darkwood was there too, with his Lightblade ignited. Thompson ignited his Lightblade too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Thompson to jump in the X-wing as the planet was destroyed. As Thompson flew his starfighter in the aftermath, he discovered that Darkwood had survived the event and watched with relief as the Worm Destroyer the man was in jumped to lightspeed before Darkwood could hurt him. However, the starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Thompson was saved by the rebels from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Thompson. Thompson told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Freedom fleet, which was now over the planet Hoth, Thompson watched as Champion spoke to Rieekan and commented he was really good. Organa claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Sullivan, but Thompson decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.[130]

Valo system

Thompson lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles, Lyle Kullan, Isla Mantra, and Ibti Myrak to accompany Blade Squadron to the Taris system to take out an Imperial shipyard. After having everyone report in, Thompson attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt the Union, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Thompson reminded Kullan that he used to be the same way. Just then, R2 revealed that multiple TIE fighters were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Thompson told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out the TIEs over comm and Thompson ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Rogue squadron escaped. Afterward, Thompson warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Thompson assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.[132]

Three days later, during a pirate attack in the Cilpar system, Myrak ignored Thompson's orders to protect a Freedom cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Thompson for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Thompson was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Thompson told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the Home One, Thompson and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying the Union was attacking Lonisa City on the planet Valo, before it cut out. Thompson ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Thompson went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the Valo system, they found no sign of the Union until Thompson got an intense feeling in The Energy and a Worm Destroyer appeared.[132]

While Thompson and Rogue Squadron fought the Union and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Thompson's orders and took her X-wing to go after him. Ackbar then tried to reach out to Thompson and let him know, but found he could not get through to Rogue Squadron. See-Threepio then warned Ackbar that he suspected Thompson's hands were full. Meanwhile, Thompson tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Antilles they had his back. However, Thompson was then caught in a tractor beam. Mantra went to save him, but Thompson warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Sullivan, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Sullivan a kill-joy. She assured Thompson he would be able to tell Sullivan she said that, but before she could take out the tractor beam, she was fired at by a fellow X-wing, who turned out to be Kullan.[131]

Kullan revealed that he had been working for the Union and would have had Thompson captured at Taris if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the tractor beams and freeing Thompson. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Antilles. Thompson then had the entire Squadron form up on Kullan as the Freedom fleet arrived. The Worm Destroyer left into hyperspace and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by the Rebels. Akbar and See-Threepio then revealed to Thompson that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Thompson assured See-Threepio that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the galaxy.[131]

On Hoth

In 3 ABY,[41] Thompson and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the Echo Base, a Freedom base on the iced planet Hoth, where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three.[11] Upon Thompson's arrival at Echo Base, Freedom leadership expected him to use The Energy to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his Lightblade, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Reyé Hollis from Freedom Alliance Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Thompson declared that he was there to help.[133]

Thompson hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Guardians sentiments gave Thompson the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Naboo name, but he could sense that Naboo was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Guardians and Thompson defended them. He also promised not to let the Naboo man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.[133]

Unused to total darkness, Thompson experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use The Energy to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Tatooine, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using The Energy and told the other man to stay still. When Thompson approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use The Energy. Hollis, recognizing that the Guardians was panicking, grabbed Thompson's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Thompson clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Thompson thought about the light and warmth of his home on Tatooine. Thompson began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the general darkness in the galaxy he faced. As their hands warmed, Thompson could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in The Energy. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Thompson recognizing that The Energy had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.[133]

While the base was still being built, two Freedom soldiers, Kes Dameron and his wife, Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by A-Wing cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son, Poe Dameron to explain why they joined the Rebellion. Thompson, however, rescued them, helped by Chester by opening a hole in the ice using his Lightblade, him, Organa, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dameron and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical droid.[134]

At some point, Thompson walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain Magna Tolvan, Vulaada Klam, and Korin Aphra, the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of The Energy-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Death Worm, which Thompson confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Chelli Aphra, Thompson told them that a Rebellion spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the Executor that someone had sabotaged the Union's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Union had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Aphra and her droid companions. Thompson revealed that sabotage had set the Union back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Aphra before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Aphra had also saved the Emperor's life.[135]

The Battle of Hoth

Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on[11] the tauntaun Riba,[136] after Sullivan had departed back to base, Thompson was attacked by a wampa, only to regain consciousness in its cave. He severed its arm with his Lightblade and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Stone, who told him to seek the teachings of Yorin in the Dagobah system.[11]

Rescued by Sullivan, Thompson recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by the medical droids 2-1B and FX-7. However, Darkwood had located Echo Base with one of his probe droids and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base,[11] thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends,[137] for the impending battle, Thompson was to lead Rogue Squadron in an airspeeder.[11] Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by Freedom journalist Corwi Selgrothe, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by Freedom trooper L'cayo Llem.[138] During the battle, Thompson led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull the AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their blasters. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then used his Lightblade to cut a hole and throw a thermal detonator. As Imperial forces breached the Rebels' defenses, the last Freedom transport departed, and Thompson fled the planet aboard his X-wing with Artoo.[11]

Traveling to Dagobah

Meeting Yorin

Thompson then traveled to Dagobah, which turned out to be a remote swamp planet, where he encountered Yorin, the former Grand Master of the fallen Guardians Order. In their initial meeting, Thompson was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Yorin, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Thompson's patience, telling him a story about three brothers,[137] and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew, which he did not find very appetizing.[11]

However, Thompson gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Yorin agreed to train Thompson only at the insistence of Stone's spirit. Thompson's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.[11]

Renewed training

Thompson's training on Dagobah lasted several weeks, with Stone needing to appear in Yorin's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Jack's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Thompson and Yorin argued, Stone also regularly appeared to stop their arguments.[139] One day during his training with Yorin, the Guardians Master had Thompson climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Thompson lost his balance and fell from the tree. Yorin decided to have Thompson carry him as he redid the task.[140]

When Yorin asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the dark side in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the armor of Victor Darkwood, meant to symbolize his inner darkness.[11] Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth, which Thompson managed to calm by leading her to her child.[137]

Leaving prematurely

Shortly after, he had a vision of Hank Sullivan and Leah Organa suffering at Cloud City on Bespin. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Thompson decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Yorin and Stone's insistence that he was not ready to face the dark side on his own yet, but Thompson was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Union.[11]

Seeing that Thompson's mind was made up, Stone let him know that if he ended up facing Darkwood, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Yorin advised him to mind what he would learned. Thompson promised to do so, and vowed to return to Dagobah one day to finish his training.[11]

Darkwood's revelation

Darkwood's trap

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform, Thompson felt Darkwood's presence,[137] and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Fett, who had come to claim a bounty put on Sullivan by Jabba the Hutt, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and Imperial officers who held Organa, Chester, and See-Threepio captive. The Princess attempted to warn Thompson that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freezing chamber where Victor Darkwood awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to the Emperor. Thompson engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce Lightblade duel.[11]

During the duel, Darkwood praised Thompson's training in The Energy thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Darkwood managed to knock Thompson into the carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Sullivan, but Thompson leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Darkwood impressed.[11]

The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's gas-processing vanes. Darkwood began using The Energy to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Guardians hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Thompson out onto the nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking the reactor shaft. Thompson managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.[11]

Darkwood re-entered the fray and forced Thompson back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Thompson was weakened, the Shadow Knights Lord demanded that Thompson surrender, lest he be destroyed as Benjamin Stone had been. Thompson refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Darkwood, to which Darkwood responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Thompson to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Thompson's hand and his Lightblade fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.[11]

A dark revelation

Seeing that Thompson was vulnerable in his weakened state, Darkwood offered to show Thompson the power of the dark side and complete his training, but Thompson adamantly refused to ever join Darkwood, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Darkwood revealed the truth: that he was his father.[11]

Horrified by Darkwood's shocking revelation, Thompson initially denounced the Shadow Knights Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Darkwood insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Darkwood further claimed that Thompson was powerful enough to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the galaxy together as father and son.[11][141] Unwilling to join Darkwood but too weak to fight back, Thompson took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Stone at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Leah Organa through The Energy, who was able to hear the call.[11]

Soon, the Millennium Falcon, with Lando Calrissian, Organa, Chester, and the droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Bespin, and the Falcon was faced with the Imperial fleet, Darkwood reached out to Thompson through The Energy, calling him "son."[11] Thompson fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Stone had not told him the truth,[137] just as the Falcon made the jump into hyperspace thanks to Artoo.[11]

Haunted by the truth

Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Thompson found his connection to The Energy considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the Falcon, after introducing Thompson to Calrissian, Organa informed Thompson of what had happened to Sullivan. Thompson wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Organa told him that there was more at stake than just Sullivan's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Freedom fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Stone, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Thompson agreeing to her plan, the Falcon jumped to hyperspace.[142]

The Falcon arrived at the Rebellion's Rendezvous Point Delta-Three in the Mid Rim to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Worm Destroyers, including the Tarkin's Will, which was under the leadership of Commander Ellian Zahra. As the Falcon entered the battle, Thompson volunteered to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Stone and Darkwood's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using The Energy to make several of the attacking TIE Fighters crash into one another. With Jack stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to hyperspace.[142]

With the fleet regrouped, Thompson found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Yorin through The Energy to receive his guidance. Angry at the Guardians Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Energy abilities. Speaking to Artoo, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Guardians. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Guardians training, and who he was meant to be if not a Guardians.[142]

As Calrissian prepared to go to Tatooine to locate Sullivan, Thompson, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Sullivan. He suggested they could meet up at the Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Calrissian was skeptical. Thompson insisted that he did not need both hands because he had The Energy, but Calrissian still insisted a rescue mission from Jabba's palace would be impossible. Organa told Thompson that he should have a prosthetic hand installed in the Nebulon-B frigate Redemption's medbay. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Thompson agreed.[143] Thompson specifically requested 2-1B, the medical droid who treated him on Hoth, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Thompson ultimately[144] received the hand as Calrissian and Chester set out to find Sullivan.[11]

An uncertain path

Return to Cloud City

Upon their return, Calrissian found Thompson training to fire a blaster with his prosthetic hand. Observing the would-be Guardians's struggles, Calrissian suggested that they return to Bespin so as to retrieve Thompson's Lightblade. As they talked, Thompson was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female hooded figure catching his Lightblade and telling him to follow his destiny.[143]

Thompson, accompanied by Calrissian, Organa, and Artoo, returned to Bespin, Calrissian assuring Thompson he knew where his Lightblade ended up. However, they were fired upon the Bespin Wing Guard upon approach, though Calrissian urged Thompson not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Thompson was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Death Worm was different when Calrissian suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Calrissian was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by stormtroopers upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the rebels split up, with Thompson and Artoo heading to the smelting chamber where Calrissian claimed his Lightblade should be located, walking past the spot where Darkwood cut off his hand. When they got there, Calrissian commed him asking for backup against stormtroopers, Thompson agreeing to help once he found his Lightblade.[145]

However, Thompson found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Calrissian it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of ugnaughts to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his Lightblade before finally deciding to search for it through The Energy. However, he instead a vivid vision of the cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Darkwood, Stone, Yorin, and the Emperor. Sensing that Organa was in trouble, he abandoned the search and found Calrissian surrounded by stormtroopers. Thompson used The Energy to push the Imperial soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Calrissian he had gotten over his block. Lando introduced his friend Lobot and Thompson revealed that he knew that Organa was in a hangar there.[146]

When Calrissian noticed he had not retrieved the Lightblade, Thompson explained that he had learned that he did not need the Lightblade to be a Guardians and that being a Guardians was about using The Energy to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Guardians, even if he never saw his father's Lightblade, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Calrissian's misgivings about his plan, Thompson was able to use The Energy to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Organa, before fighting off stormtroopers to get to his X-wing and take off from Bespin with it. He commed Organa and asked to temporarily part with the Rebels to find someone The Energy was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After R2 located the planet from Thompson's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.[146]

While Thompson traveled to find the woman from his visions, Darkwood followed a false lead from an Imperial officer to Thompson, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a droid named 'Ardee' helped him destroy the Death Worm. Darkwood killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Thompson's name would die. He told the Imperial officer to verify his findings next time and said Thompson could still be anywhere.[147]

Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Thompson arrived at the planet Serelia, where he encountered the woman from the vision at a dock on the coastline. However, when he asked whether she was a Guardians, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Thompson gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched, Thompson attempted hold back the rising water with The Energy, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.[147]

Verla informed Thompson that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Guardians, cynically telling him about Order 66, the Inquisitorius and Victor Darkwood himself. Sensing Thompson's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Darkwood. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him.[147]Fortunately, Thompson's life was saved by Artoo, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.[148]

When she regained consciousness, Thompson freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a Lightblade, as she had given up on The Energy and refused to take part in the conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Darkwood's name was Anakin Thompson, and that she had learned from her master Ferren Barr that Anakin had once been a hero of the Clone Copulation but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the Inquisitors and Darkwood and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Thompson's belief that The Energy had led her to him and insisted that Energy only used people.[148]

Tempes

Thompson still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering galaxy and believed she could lead him to a Lightblade. Although she strongly recommended that Thompson should leave The Energy behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a Guardians outpost from the High Republic Era on the Outer Rim astronomical object of Tempes where she had once sought artifacts of the Guardians. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Thompson thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.[148]

Thompson flew to Tempes and was almost struck down by the planet's powerful lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring R2 and land safely, venturing to the outpost in the rain. He and his droid entered after he realized he needed to use The Energy to open the door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a new yellow-bladed Lightblade but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Darkwood, who had turned the outpost into a trap for Guardians and Energy-sensitives. After the Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning Lightblade Lightblade, Thompson resolved to not let him kill a Guardians again and used the yellow Lightblade to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.[148]

The Inquisitor taunted the young Guardians's skill with a Lightblade, but Thompson acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Guardians. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the Lightblade and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Guardians Order was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of the Guardians Order and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his saber, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Darkwood arrived.[148]

Thompson returned to the Freedom fleet and reunited with Organa. Organa implored him to show the assorted rebels what he had found, and so Thompson activated his new Lightblade, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the Rebellion.[148]

Around this time, after a long crusade, Darkwood discovered that the location where his late wife Padmé Amidala had given birth to Thompson was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.[149]

Battle at Elessia

Somewhere in the Inner Rim, Thompson and Organa met with Commander Grek and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against the Union. See-Threepio was concerned that this would alert the Union to their location, but Thompson told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.[151]

The Union's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances Fourth and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in the Inner Rim, so Zahra initiated an attack that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike Energy led an attack on the Alliance's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike Energy attempted to attack Leah Organa's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before the strike Energy arrived. The Imperial forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. TIE interceptors created a blockade of Home One's hangar bay, that prevented Alliance starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.[150]

Shortly into the battle, the Fourth Division's ships emerged from hyperspace and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Jack Thompson, who assumed the callsign Red Leader. Thompson was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey (Green Three), along with Lando Calrissian and Chester in the Millennium Falcon. While fighting, Organa contacted him to check in, Thompson informing her that the Imperials were pinning down the Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Organa abruptly ended the communication, Thompson called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on Home One's blockade, to push the TIEs back, while those starfighters who were trapped in the hangar bays prepared to launch, including Wedge Antilles (Red Two). As soon as Thompson cleared the TIEs, all the Alliance starfighters aboard Home One, including Antilles, launched to repay the favor.[150]

Thompson eventually left the space battle to board the frigate where Leah Organa was under attack from an Imperial boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Thompson, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Darkwood's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Thompson and Organa went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off the Union. There, Thompson noticed something was off with Organa, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.[150]

Searching for Sullivan

Around this time, Darkwood had his armor damaged during a mission. Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Darkwood wondered if Thompson's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Darkwood suspected it had not and considered Thompson weak, believing the fledgling Guardians had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Darkwood believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Thompson he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Hank Sullivan.[153] In killing Thompson Darkwood believed he would kill Benjamin Stone's last hope and prove those who thought Thompson would be his end wrong.[154] While tracking Sullivan, Darkwood's partner, a Shadow Knights assassin named Ochi, figured out that Darkwood planned to lure Thompson to him with Sullivan.[155]

While practicing his Lightblade and Energy skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Yorin's training, Thompson was interrupted by See-Threepio, insisting he did not mind the distraction. See-Threepio said that Artoo wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his new Lightblade. Before Artoo could say anything, Chester arrived and revealed, via See-Threepio translating, that Boba Fett had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Thompson to come with him to rescue Sullivan from him. Chester shot down Thompson's suggestion that they bring Calrissian, saying he was not to be trusted and that Sullivan would not want to see him, but Thompson agreed. Thompson, See-Threepio, Artoo, and Chester then took the Falcon to Nar Shaddaa, though R2 complained to See-Threepio on the way that he had not been able to speak to Thompson. Thompson was annoyed that Organa had to stay behind to coordinate Operation Starlight and urged Chester to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the planet and what had happened with Grakkus the Hutt.[152]

On the planet, Thompson watched as Chester met with his contact Sagwa, See-Threepio explaining that he and Chester used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a new victor in the Garkata Fighting Arena was Boba Fett. Looking at a hologram of the winner, Thompson also thought they looked like Boba but noticed it was labeled "Jango." Thompson spoke with a Pa'lowick attendant at the Arena to get information on "Jango" but was annoyed by both the Pa'lowick's refusal to answer questions without payment and See-Threepio spouting the mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Jango," used an alarm to call the Kanji gang, who were upset that "Jango" had defeated their fighter in the arena. Using his Lightblade to fight back against the thugs, who attacked despite Thompson's warnings, Thompson and the others fled, though Chester was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagwa through the streets before stealing and fleeing on a speeder, they encountered many more groups of Kanji that Thompson fended off with The Energy and his Lightblade until Artoo picked them up in the Falcon. However, some of the Kanji planned to report seeing Thompson to Imperials on Vandor.[152]

Afterward, R2 finally gave his message to Thompson via See-Threepio, revealing that he had found a list of former Guardians outposts across the galaxy in his databanks that he seemingly acquired on the Death Worm. This greatly excited Thompson, who eagerly wanted to learn more, feeling like The Energy was providing for him and making R2 list the locations for him immediately. Sagwa then entered with a message, See-Threepio translating, in which he revealed that Organa had received a message from an unknown party claiming to be in possession of Sullivan.[152] Though Organa was starting to lose hope,

Ab Dalis

Thompson and the others met up with Organa, who told them that Fett did not have Sullivan anymore and that he was going to be sold in a auction in the attendance of most of the galaxy's crime syndicates on Jekara.[157] Though Organa was starting to lose hope, Thompson assured her that they would find him.[158] After Organa revealed Sullivan was in the possession of Crimson Dawn, who Chester remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Thompson expressed his concerns to Organa about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from the heart. Organa agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take the mission to get Sullivan back. Later Thompson joined Antilles and Starlight Squadron in his X-wing, having promised he would join them for a patrol, but assured Organa he would join her on Jekara soon.[157]

Eager to get the fleet back together, and knowing recon missions were dicey, Thompson told Antilles that he was happy to join the mission to find and recover a fleet division. Hoping to boost pilot L'ulo L'ampar's morale, he also revealed to the squadron that the Alliance was close to recovering Captain Sullivan. After traveling through hyperspace to Ab Dalis, Freyta Smyth and Antilles explained that it was chosen as a Freedom rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a cataclysmic impact make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier. Mart Mattin and Thompson then received a broken transmission, which R2 unscrambled, revealing it was from the Eleventh Division of the Rendezvous Point Lambda-Four at Ab Dalis who were being attacked by Imperial forces. The rebels on the surface, learning that heroes of Yavin Thompson and Antilles were above them, became desperate for the squadron to come down and help them.[156]

Approaching the planet, Antilles advised them all to target ground targets and Thompson relayed a message from R2 that TIE fighters were approaching them. Hearing that Thompson, who had made the trench run on the Death Worm was among the fighters, one Freedom who had conducted a geological survey of the planet sent coordinates to the squadron for a ground target beneath the Worm Destroyer. They were unsure about what the target was, but after scanning and determining it was a tunnel that could have been hiding someone important, Antilles sent Thompson to hit it, comparing it to making the Death Worm run. Thompson started to make the run while the others kept TIEs off of him, feeling like he was making the Death Worm run again, but he started to have a flashback to the battle of Yavin that instead ended with Darkwood taking down the Falcon and saying he would claim Sullivan for himself, disturbing Thompson and causing him to miss the target. However, the rest of Starlight Squadron were on the same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a volcanic explosion that destroyed the Worm Destroyer that attacked the Eleventh Division. Afterward, Thompson apologized for missing the shot, before the others realized they had rescued Mon Mothma. Thompson then told Antilles that he was going to Jekara to help Organa.[156]

Auction of Hank Sullivan

Haunted

Thompson contacted Oragna before he left for Jekara but only received a message from her saying things got complicated before losing signal. Unsure whether Organa could still hear him, he warned that Darkwood was potentially coming after Sullivan, having realized that his vision on Ab Dalis might have been a warning and a premonition.[156] This premonition also made him panic as he did not believe he could beat him. In hyperspace on the way to Jekara, Thompson recalled fighting Darkwood on Cloud City and the Shadow Knights Lord's utter brutality, strength, and ineffable power, likening it to a gravel storm. Imagining himself as he was at that moment fighting Darkwood on Cloud City with his yellow Lightblade, Thompson began to believe he could be a storm too, but then just thought he was kidding himself and that Yorin and Darkwood had been right about him. He began to accept the fact that he would have to face Darkwood on Jekara to protect his friends but did not feel ready. Desperate for guidance, he tried to reach out to The Energy to speak to Stone, but his fear made it difficult to access.[159]

Just as he was about to exit hyperspace, Organa asked Thompson for help in distracting Darkwood because the Shadow Knights Lord arrived at the auction, but he was quickly occupied dealing with Darkwood's Super Worm Destroyer the Executor that was over Jekara. Overwhelmed, Thompson fought off various TIE fighters before leading them close to and getting their shots to hit the ship of one of the crime syndicates present at the auction, the Son-tuul Pride, which made the syndicate retaliate against the Union as Thompson hoped it would. Finally given a window to approach the planet, he told Organa he was approaching as she told him to hurry. However, the Imperials established a secure channel between him and Darkwood, who had sensed that he was there in The Energy. Darkwood threatened to kill the frozen Hank if he did not arrive to face him,[159] while imagining fighting Thompson and thinking that he would not be a father to him or let him hide behind his friends.[160] After hearing the message, Thompson, haunted by the image of his current self being killed by Darkwood on Cloud City, decided he was not ready to face him and turned his X-wing around.[159]

The chase

As he left, Thompson explained to Organa his belief that Darkwood wanted to turn him, and that if he fought Darkwood, he would kill them too.[160] He told her he would help from his ship and then taunted Darkwood, urging the Shadow Knights Lord to pursue him. Organa revealed that his stunt had worked and Darkwood was coming for him, Thompson telling her that he hoped to keep him busy and imploring her to save Sullivan.[161] As he assured Organa he would be fine, the starship of a criminal group harpooned his fighter and started to pull him in. He was freed by laser blast's from Darkwood's TIE fighter.[160] As the two engaged in a dogfight, Organa contacted Thompson again, who simply repeated his earlier sentiments and said he needed to focus.[161] He then tried to lure Darkwood into open space, believing the Shadow Knights Lord did not want to kill him, but Darkwood shot his X-wing anyway, Thompson realizing that Darkwood did want to kill him as he crash-landed on Jekara.[160]

After urging Artoo to repair the ship quickly, he used a blaster to shoot at Darkwood's TIE fighter as it approached. However, Imperial Administrator Sly Moore, who had been tasked with killing Darkwood dropped a droid gang she hired called the Droid Crush Pirates of Bestoon onto Darkwood's TIE and near Thompson. Thompson initally ignited his Lightblade and acted defensively, but the droids instead fixed his fighter, leaving Thompson confused. Thompson, accepting of the help, flew away with Droid Crush members riding his wing and shooting at Darkwood.[160]
