Opting to fly away to the planet's Polar Sea to get Darkwood to follow him, Thompson briefly thought he had lost Darkwood until Darkwood's TIE fighter emerged from the water beneath him. Darkwood taunted him, saying his increased strength in The Energy would not save him and that he had Sullivan, so he would not be saving Thompson either. Noticing how quickly the sea's water was freezing, Thompson trapped Darkwood in the ice by making a giant wave of water with his X-wing and leading Darkwood into it when it quickly froze. Despite having the perfect opportunity to shoot at and kill Darkwood, Thompson could not do it after what he had revealed at Cloud City, but knew he would eventually need to face him. Thompson reached out to Organa on the Falcon who explained that they and the Imperial shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they could not reach Sullivan but Sullivan could not be taken to the flagship. Thompson said he was on his way and assured them Darkwood would not be a threat for a while. With Hutt fighters now chasing the shuttle with Sullivan on it, Organa tried to get Thompson to help, but Darkwood emerged from the ice in his TIE fighter and dragged Thompson back into a fight, so Thompson told her he could not help.[162]

Darkwood soon chased Thompson back into space. With Darkwood reveling in making Thompson feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Thompson cried out as the shuttle containing Sullivan was fired at by Hutt and Imperial fighters.[160] Thankfully, Darkwood was soon ordered by the Emperor to assist with a Hutt attack that was happening against the Union, which forced the Shadow Knights Lord to leave his son behind, not before reminding Thompson that he was no Guardians. Thompson insisted that when they met again he would be a Guardians.[163] Darkwood resented having to leave but was proud of terrifying Thompson, recognizing that the Emperor wanted Thompson to live and was orchestrating things so that it could be the case.[164] During his confrontation with Darkwood, Organa and the rest failed to rescue Hank Sullivan, who was captured by Boba Fett once again after the auction.[163]

In search of Guardians secrets

After the breakout of the Syndicate War, the Freedom Alliance had a meeting with many of its leaders that Jack Thompson was present for. As Admiral Ackbar discussed the Alliance's next moves in its rebuilding effort, Leah Organa noticed that Thompson was distant. He explained that Artoo had given him a list of possible Guardians sites across the galaxy from an Imperial archive. He expressed that he knew he was needed by the Alliance, but that he felt like he should be trying to continue growing as a Guardians. He recognized that he would need to face Darkwood again and wanted to be ready when he did. Organa told him that it was okay and that he should trust what The Energy was telling him, offering to smooth things over with Mon Mothma and telling him that they could fight the battles without him for now.[165]

Thompson consulted Artoo for the list of Guardians sites and worried that since it was an imperial list all of the places would have been destroyed or looted. But he recognized that he needed to keep going if he was going to face Darkwood, though he wished Master Yorin would talk to him. Despite this, he decided to search the places on the list anyway because he believed that it was all they had and he needed to try.[165]

He visited the planet Ilum first and discovered the mass imperial blockade and presence there and the Union's exploitation of the planet. He felt in The Energy that the planet was important, but also felt that now only held pain as the Union was killing it more and more every day. He believed there was nothing for him there and he and Artoo used the weather of the planet to leave and slip past the blockade. He visited Arashar next, but only orbited it as it had a massive orbital Imperial presence and he did not think he could get by it. He then visited Lothal, and traveled to the former location of the Lothal Guardians Temple. He could feel that it needed to be opened with The Energy, but it did not respond to him and felt like it had been buried or locked. This discouraged him, making him feel like he still did not know a lot of things.[165]

A legacy on Al'doleem

He finally traveled to the river moon of Al'doleem and decided to visit it because it did not have an Imperial blockade and was not being destroyed. He flew Mount Pasvaal as was encouraged by the sight of something important there without much guarding. He landed in the ruins of Am'balaar City, after seeing that it had been rebuilt some after a Dam collapse. He told Artoo that they would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of the Union's biggest targets. He walked up to two stormtroopers and tried to perform a Guardians mind trick on them that Yorin had taught him and he had seen Benjamin Stone do, asking the troopers to tell him what the Union was doing at the top of the mountain, but the mind trick failed and the troopers merely told him to move along.[165]

Thompson and Artoo aimlessly wandered the city until R2 noticed a hull panel from a Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light interceptor and stopped him. Thompson did not understand until a man named Colli walked out and told Thompson that he was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships. Colli explained that the Guardians flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in the Clone Copulation. Colli told Thompson to come inside, as he had noticed the Lightblade on his belt, and warned that if he had others might have too. Colli asked him if he was really a Guardians, to which Thompson explained that he was trying to be. Colli told him that he had known a Guardians named Kirak Infil'a who had worked in the monastery on top of Mount Pasvaal and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his Aetherspire when the city was attacked and destroyed. He explained that both Infil'a and his parents had died. Colli asked Thompson about his story, but Artoo tried to stop him from saying anything. Colli insisted that he was not going to turn them into the Imperials and that helping Thompson would probably be the closest he could get to repaying his debt to the Guardians that saved him. Thompson said that he needed to learn as much about the Guardians as he could from what of their legacy remained and that he believed the mountain that Infil'a had come from was connected to the Order and that he wanted to figure out what the Union was looking for there.[165]

Colli took Thompson to the Monastery at the top of the mountain, but explained that there were also tunnels and chambers in the mountain that the Guardians had used and that the Union was bringing artifacts out of it all the time. Thompson considered using his Lightblade and The Energy to let go and cut right through the Imperial forces in his way but could not bring himself to do it and instead gave his Lightblade to R2 for safekeeping. He went and meditated with The Energy on the side of the mountain before returning to the warehouse where Colli had told him the Union stored the artifacts they found in the mountain. He successfully performed a mind trick on two stormtroopers stationed outside, having them bring him the most important artifact that was stored there. They brought him a holocron, which Jack recognized from his experience with Grakkus the Hutt. He reached out with The Energy to unlock it as he had learned how to do and found a recording of Master Yorin teaching lessons.[165] Thompson was disappointed, however, that the lesson Yorin taught in the holocron was one that he had already taught him back on Dagobah. Burdened by the series of setbacks he had faced recently, Thompson considered dropping the holocron off of a cliff, but was stopped by R2. Thompson opened the holocron again, and this time Yorin discussed Vergences in The Energy, listing the Guardians Temple on Coruscant, Jedha, and the Living Sea of Gazian as examples. Jack had never heard of the Living Sea of Gazian and decided to go to the planet to check it out.[166]

The Living Sea of Gazian

Arriving in the Gazian system, Jack was excited by the possibility of finding a Guardians-related site that the Union did not know about. He landed on a platform he found on the planet, but was disappointed to only find a sea of mushrooms. However he could feel the strength of The Energy there and decided to look around, but when he walked onto the surface, he fell into a mushroom-filled cavity below. His breath mask was pulled off of him and he went unconscious, starting to have a vision where he was wearing the clothes he wore during the Duel on Cloud City and was in possession of the Thompson Lightblade. He then saw a man in High Republic Era Guardians robes sitting next to him, who then took the Thompson Lightblade with Energy and admired it, comparing it to his own. Thompson held up a blaster and told the man to give it back. He acquiesced but commented that he was not scared of the blaster. The man introduced himself as Guardians Master Elzar Mann. He asked Thompson if he was a Guardians, to which Thompson said that he was trying to be but was still learning about The Energy and trying to understand it. Mann told him that that never really stops, as the Guardians Order goes back a long way and there was a lot to learn.[166]

Just then, their surroundings shifted to Ahch-To, the birthplace of the Guardians. Thompson asked Mann what exactly was going on. Mann explained that Gazian was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Mann added that he had come to the planet to get answers after having a vision, and that was why his imprint was there. Thompson explained that the Republic had fallen and an Union had taken over. He said that he had never met a Guardians like Mann, and had only ever met Benjamin Stone and Yorin, the latter of whom Mann remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Thompson had also said that he had met a Guardians named Anakin Thompson, but that he had fallen to the Dark side, something that Mann knew happened occasionally.[166]

Mann then showed Thompson an imprint of what the High Republic looked like, telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after the defeat of Victor Bane. The mention of Victor Bane made Thompson confused and comment that it was all too much. Thompson elaborated that when he started his journey it was exciting and he had been a hero, then he was supposed to save the galaxy by learning from Yorin, but then it all fell apart and now he felt that if he did it wrong he would become a monster. But Mann said that The Energy was so powerful and boundless so it should not be easy to learn how to use it. Mann recalled when he had trained to be a Guardians and how even with help and support how impossible everything felt. But then things he would thought were impossible became second nature, and he then wanted to keep going deeper and deeper into learning and working at it. But Thompson was alone, and felt that it was too much. Mann said that the Guardians tend to be what they need to be, whether that be warriors fighting evil, teachers teaching the galaxy, and sometimes they were almost nothing, left with the responsibility of being the only light in the dark of the galaxy. Suddenly they were underwater, floating in an expansive ocean. Mann then told Thompson that he knew the kind of pressure that The Energy choosing you could bring, and decided to tell him a piece of advice that he did not think many other Guardians would tell him: He did not have to be a Guardians. Mann told him that there were other ways to use The Energy safely and protect life and light, and that there are many paths and choices that could be true to the person he wanted to be. But he also told Thompson that if he wanted it, the Guardians were there for him. Other past Guardians then appeared in the water and floated around them until they found themselves back in the void-like vision of the Living Sea. He asked Thompson if he wanted to stay there and speak to him and the many minds in the Living Sea, but Thompson told him that he thought he should leave. Mann was disappointed, but reminded Thompson that a part of him would remain there to talk to them as Thompson saw an imprint of himself appear in front of him.[166]

Thompson came to, ignited his Lightblade, and put his breath mask back on. He then saw many bodies of Guardians that had stayed in the Living Sea. He found one Guardians skeleton holding a sacred Guardians text, which he brought to the surface and recognized was an invitation.[166]

Zahra later referenced her meeting with Thompson to Bey, as she believed Bey to the most arrogant Freedom she had ever met next to Thompson.[167]

Unity Day attack

After he returned to the Alliance, Thompson took part in an attack on the Imperial Unity Day Parade on Bar'leth, one of the Rebels' first large scale operations since the Battle of Hoth. Organa and the rest of the Alliance hoped it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back against the Union, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Thompson flew in his X-Wing as part of Red Squadron during the attack. After destroying the Union's vast artillery on display in the parade, Thompson told the others over comm that Artoo had detected a wave of TIE fighters launching from a nearby Imperial airbase outside of the city and suggested they leave. Wedge Antilles, flying as Red Leader, acknowledged Thompson and told everyone to follow him, leading all of the Alliance fighters to a successful escape.[168]

Afterward, Thompson participated in a toast to the Freedom Alliance lead by Antilles before having a drink and sitting down. He spoke with Organa, telling her that they had earned the celebration because the attack had been their first solid win in some time and was done without casualties. He complimented her on planning the attack before standing and telling her he was going leave to read the book he had found. He explained that it was full of incredible things and that he could not stop thinking about it, adding that even holding it made him feel more connected to The Energy. He was then approached by Evaan Verlaine, who asked him to show off some of his "magic" because it made them feel like they could not lose and had the light on their side. He was reluctant to do so because it made him feel like a show-off, but he agreed, igniting his yellow Lightblade. Verlaine called it magic while many looked on in awe.[168]

The second Death Worm revealed

Meanwhile, Crimson Dawn agents Bevelyn and Jon Melton received a message from Lady Qi'ra instructing them to cause chaos for the Union. Because they were deep undercover at the Union's secret Death Worm II, they decided that they should expose the facility to the Freedom Alliance.[168] They managed to escape with their children and get a message to the Rebels telling them that they had top secret Imperial weapons information they would give in exchange for protection, but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by General Hera Syndulla at an intelligence briefing that Thompson was present at among many other intelligence hits. Thompson asked about it and Syndulla explained to him what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While Thompson was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.[170]

Later, in a separate room, Syndulla asked Thompson if he truly believed there was something significant about the defectors and wondered if he was sensing something due to his connection with The Energy. Jack said that he has learned everything is connected to The Energy, Syndulla responding that he sounded like Kanan Jarrus, her late Guardians lover. Thompson asked if that was a good thing, and she said it usually was. Commodore Grek decided that anything that Thompson thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up information about the case to walk Thompson through it. While they only had vague information, the aide informed Thompson that a relay station, one of many they had in out-of-the-way spots where the fleet could send communications safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be imperial defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for information about a classified weapons site, though the Alliance lost connection after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because many information hits turn out to be imperial traps and are therefore not worth the risk.[169]

They had also taken the shuttle's signature and learned that the defectors had escaped in an Imperial shuttle called the Scopium and discovered right then while checking the databases that the Union had put a system-wide alert on the shuttle, suggesting the defectors' legitimacy. Grek asked Thompson if he could give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Thompson could not, explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention and find the shuttle. They discovered that the last place the shuttle's signal had popped up was on Coruscant, and so Thompson suggested he go there. Grek and Syndulla told him it was a bad idea, the former reminding him that the Emperor lives there and the latter suggesting that they send pathfinders to mount a rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for him across the galaxy, and because he was the only Guardians they had.[169]

Thompson understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that the Emperor and Darkwood were off-world, even confirming it with the aide who said they were on Darkwood's flagship in the Mid Rim and that he could do things that others could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said. Grek said he saw his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again. Syndulla emphasized that Coruscant was the most dangerous place for a Freedom to be in the galaxy and asked if he had a plan. Thompson said he had the start of a plan which was to hide the fact that he was a Freedom by dressing as an Imperial.[169]

Thompson eventually arrived in the Works District of Coruscant, two Freedom agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Imperial Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of the Rebels telling him he looked good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at the outfit. They both told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but Thompson assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine. He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a pickup, admitting that he did not know exactly where the defectors were and it could be intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his Lightblade to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if people saw it they would report the Guardians bounty and he would be swarmed with Imperials. When they asked him, Thompson revealed that he was going to track down the defectors by speaking to stormtroopers.[169]

Eventually Thompson tracked the Meltons to a sewer, but they immediately started shooting at him until he told them he was there to help and was with was with the Alliance, forcing him to use The Energy to jump around the sewer to dodge the blasts. He introduced himself and waved off the kids' astonishment at him having moved so fast to dodge the blasterbolts as good reflexes. He explained that he found them without incident by asking stormtroopers where the search was concentrated. He checked in with the kids and asked if they were okay and they said that their parents were keeping them safe, which did not surprise Thompson. Coruscant Police then arrived, and Thompson told them to stand down and that he would take it from there, but they questioned this as they had not been told any officers would be down there and asked him for authorization. Believing Thompson could not take on the troopers on his own, Bevelyn and Jon made a plan for her to take the kids and run while he held them off, but Thompson overheard them and assured them that it would not be necessary. As the police pointed their blasters at him and he raised his hand to reach out with The Energy, he admitted that there were too many of them for him to fight or mind trick, but told them not to worry because he could sense help all around them. He then used The Energy to call down many Corridor ghouls, beastly creatures that lived beneath Coruscant, down through the ceiling of the sewer and had them attack the Police, Thompson saying "told you" to the Meltons as they took the opportunity to run away and escape.[169]

Thompson got the Meltons back safely for them to debrief their intelligence people, but they talked to him on the way. He gathered Freedom leaders Ackbar, Syndulla, and Mothma and told them that he needed to tell them what the Meltons told him. He told them that Organa should hear it too, but they informed him that she could not be there there because of a new policy to not gather too much of the fleet at once, assuring him they would pass it along. Thompson revealed that the defectors were Crimson Dawn agents that were deep undercover as a cargo specialist and aide to a commander of a secret weapons facility. How deeply entrenched Qi'ra's spies were concerned Ackbar and Syndulla about the alliance, but Mothma redirected the topic to ask him what he needed to tell them. Thompson revealed that the Union was building another Death Worm. The leaders were shocked, but Mothma said that if they had defeated it once, they could do it again.[169]

At some point after this, Thompson sensed the activation and attempted opening of the Fermata Cage, an ancient Shadow Knights device, by members of Crimson Dawn.[171]

Search for the Kezarat convoy

Later, Thompson took a break with Organa, Calrissian, Lobot, Chester, and Amilyn Holdo at the latter's friend's house on the planet Spira. Sitting by the pool, Thompson read the Guardians text he had recovered from Gazian. Calrissian, who was playing Sabacc with Lobot and Chester tried to convince Thompson to stop reading and join their game, but Thompson refused, explaining that he did not often get the opportunity to study and wanted to use the time to focus since he was learning so much about The Energy and the Guardians from the text. Eventually, Calrissian pushed Chester into the pool, sending a wave toward Thompson that he held back and redirected with The Energy while still reading with a smile. Meanwhile, Holdo tried to convince Organa to relax especially since they were by handsome men. Realizing she was referring to Thompson and Calrissian, Organa was unimpressed, but Holdo said that she would look at them if Organa would not.[172]

Later, Thompson, Organa, and Holdo attended an auction on Spira for an ancient Nihil strikeship, the real reason Holdo had wanted them to come to the planet. They were all dressed in elegant masks to hide their identities. As Holdo explained to Organa that they needed the strikeship to find a set of tankerships full of fuel to solve the Rebels' fuel problems, Thompson felt something that made him groan and put his head in his hands. Holdo was losing the auction to the curator of the Imperial Museum, so she asked Thompson if he could use The Energy to get the man to stop bidding. Thompson said that he could not perform a mind trick because he was feeling off, believing something was happening with the dark side of The Energy that was distracting him,[172] which unbeknownst to Thompson was a disturbance in The Energy caused by the opening of the Fermata Cage.[173] Thompson agreed to try the trick anyway, but Calrissian commed them and told him not to. Organa, holding Thompson's arm to stop him from doing anything, listened as Calrissian explained that the mind trick would draw too much attention to them, and that the man might know about The Energy and figure out what happened since he ran the Imperial Museum. Calrissian suggested they instead let the man win.[172]

Later, in a hangar where the man who won the strikeship was keeping it, Thompson ignited his Lightblade and drew a bunch of stormtroopers that were guarding the ship to him, deflecting their blaster shots back at them. While they were distracted by Thompson, Calrissian and Holdo fired on them from above and Chester stopped some of the troopers from locking off the ship. Once all the stormtroopers were taken down, Calrissian said that he knew his plan would work because all the stormtroopers would be so caught up in taking down the Guardians to get a reward that they would not notice anything else. Thompson reminded Calrissian that it might not have ended well for him, but Calrissian claimed he knew he would be able to handle it because of the book he had been studying. Holdo asked Thompson to cut open the side of the strikeship with his Lightblade and make a circular hole not too deep. After Thompson did, they retrieved the Path engine from inside the ship.[172]

Later, back at Holdo's friend's house, Holdo explained that she was planning to go after the Kezarat convoy, which Organa had suspected. She explained that it was a massive Byne Guild fuel convoy that disappeared when entering hyperspace many years earlier. Thompson asked if it was because it got hit by raiders because it was such a sought-after target, but Holdo revealed it actually disappeared during a storm in hyperspace caused by the Nihil. She hoped that the last message sent by the convoy that was thought to be nonsense was actually navigational data designed only to communicate with a Path engine and that both together could take them to the convoy. After Chester finished installing the Path engine into their ship the Skyfarer, a group of TIE fighters found them and started going after them. Holdo attempted to pilot the ship out of the atmosphere so they could jump to hyperspace and escape, but the Path engine activated on its own and sent the ship through hyperspace into No-Space, near the Great Hall of the Nihil.[172]

The lost colony

Thompson and his friends did not recognize No-Space and had no idea where they were, but Thompson expressed that while he had not traveled as much as they had, he knew the galaxy had strange things in it. He added that The Energy seemed off and distant, as if there was not much around that was alive. After Holdo tried and failed to take the ship to hyperspace again, Thompson suggested that they needed to disconnect the Path engine to see if it would fix the problem, but Holdo revealed they would need to land somewhere first. After landing on the Great Hall, Lando lamented the dangers of falling off the edge there and said it was worse than Cloud City, but Thompson insisted that Cloud City had been really bad. A child in a spacesuit then appeared and asked them if they had 'flavors', which Calrissian interpreted to mean the kid wanted to eat them. Thompson said he was just a kid, but Calrissian asked him to remind him to tell Thompson about the White Worms gang's savage children. The kid eventually advised them to hide and ran off, and Thompson sensed with The Energy that the kid was terrified and did not know where he was going. The kid then jumped off the edge of the Hall, which shocked Thompson, but he just activated a jetpack and flew away.[175]

Lando suggested they get out of there, which Thompson agreed with, suggesting they go back to their ship and try to find the other place there that the boy mentioned. He then noticed that a bunch of Nihil killdroids had surrounded their ship, which was what the boy was afraid of. Thompson began to fight them with his Lightblade, but quickly realized that they were Lightblade resistant. A ship then pulled up next to the hall, and Holdo suggested that they board it to escape the droids, but was concerned by having to jump across open space to it. Thompson insisted that it was not that bad of a jump and because the ramp had a magnetic shield they would not be in the vacuum very long, but Calrissian implied that easy for him to say because he was a Guardians. They all jumped over to the ship without issue, except for Calrissian, who tripped and fell, prompting Thompson to use The Energy to pull him back up while muttering "I can do this." Once Calrissian was safe he thanked Thompson for saving his life and assured him he would not bother him about studying his book ever again. Thompson revealed that he could have gotten him to safety faster but could not because there was still something going on with The Energy that he described as ripples messing with his concentration. Once the ship they were on docked with a larger ship, a voice over a speaker ordered them to throw their weapons out into space or they would vent them out into space. Thompson considered using his lightaber to just cut through the door into the bigger ship but realized they would just vent them anyway. He threw his Lightblade out into space, which he said hurt because they were hard to find, but the kid with the jetpack just flew over, picked it up, and ignited it before flying away, Thompson warning him that he needed to be careful with it or he would hurt himself.[175]

The voice over the speaker welcomed them to the Colony of Kezarat. Holdo yelled that they just needed to go home, but an Abyssin left the cockpit and told them they were home.[175] After Organa said they would not attack and were listening, the individual introduced himself as Captain Blythe, and Holdo introduced herself, Thompson, and the others to him. Blythe then began a tour of the colony, confirming that it was the old convoy and the fuel was still there. The kid, who Blythe revealed was named Forvan, then ran up excitedly, and Blythe explained that he had gotten excited from seeing a Lightblade. Thompson asked if it was because they had Guardians there and asked for his saber back, but Blythe said they did not have Guardians there and he would get his Lightblade back in due time. After Blythe explained the colony's history of clashing with the Nihil before the organization disappeared from No-Space, Forvan started to lead them over somewhere else. Blythe told Thompson to let Forvan down easy, and when he said he did not understand, Blythe said he would.[174]

Forvan took them to mural depicting a Guardians. Thompson was shocked that they had a Guardians with the convoy, but Blythe said she had not been there from the beginning and just found her way to No-Space. Blythe explained that her name was Alareen Xie, and that after she died a belief spread within the colony that a Guardians would someday lead them home. Blythe once again reiterated for Thompson to let Forvan down easy, so he bent down to speak with Forvan and told him that while the Guardians could do incredible things, he could not assure them that he would get them out of No-Space, though he would at least do everything he could to try and get them out of there or to make their lives better. Forvan once again said that Guardians could do anything, so Thompson just said he would try his best. After Blythe warned them that they would be there for a while and would not be able to get back to their ship, Thompson and the others all went to sleeping quarters given to them. Thompson paced around the room and Organa recognized something was wrong and asked him about it. Thompson explained that he had to get out of there to get back to the ship to get the irreplaceable Guardians text that he had left on it. When she brought it up, Thompson said he was not worried about his Lightblade as he could get another one, but that without the text he would not be able to move forward with his training. He feared that if he lost it and was no longer able to access its knowledge, techniques, and history of The Energy and the Guardians Order or be able to use it to find places he could learn more, he would never truly become a Guardians.[174]

Escape from No-Space

After Chester realized that they could use the Path engine to escape No-Space, Blythe gathered Thompson and the others, offering them an old Longbeam cruiser to retrieve it. Thompson was skeptical that it could fly, but Blythe assured them that it used to and just needed to be fixed up. Once they got the cruiser to take off and leave the colony, Holdo and Lando took inventory of what they had, including that they had a Guardians. Thompson reminded them that his connection to The Energy was different and hard to use in No-Space and something strange felt like it was happening to it anyway, but he assured them he would try his best. Calrissian assured them they would be fine, but Thompson was still skeptical and pointed out that the killdroids had found and taken the Path engine. Thompson decided that he would distract the droids so the others could get the engine, but after needed to go back to the Skyfarer and get his Guardians text, refusing to leave without it. He added that he had talked to Forvan before they left and that the boy, wanting to help out a Guardians, gave him specialized gear, including a spacesuit, that he could use to get down to the Great Hall. Wearing the spacesuit, Thompson jumped from the cruiser down to the Hall, ignited his Lightblade, and called out to the droids, encouraging them to go after him. As Thompson, Holdo, and Calrissian distracted the droids, Thompson deflecting their blastershots with his Lightblade, Chester went after the engine. However, some droids noticed and went after Chester anyway, so Thompson sent them flying away off into no-Space with The Energy. He warned Chester that he probably would not be able to do it again, so he needed to run and get the engine back to the ship.[176]

After shooting a droid behind Chester with a blaster, Thompson started to run off and told Holdo to keep Chester safe while he ran after the Guardians text. She reminded him that his Lightblade could not get through their armor and that it was just a book, but Thompson said that it was more than just a book and that he had not been focused when his Lightblade would not cut them the first time, but now was. Thompson sliced through the droids with his Lightblade while saying "The Energy is with me" and calling out for what they did with his book. He eventually found the book on a ledge at the edge of the Hall and decided that he would easily be able to grab it and fly back to the ship, but a killdroid shot his jetpack, sending him flying uncontrollably toward the book and leaving it without fuel. As killdroids approached him, their footsteps shaking the ground caused the book to go over the edge. Thompson screamed in horror and reached out to it with his right hand, which was holding his Lightblade, but a droid grabbed his hand and lifted him up by it, crushing both his hand and his Lightblade.[176] The droid threw him onto the ground and into the remains of the Skyfarer, and Thompson cried out in horror when he saw his hand and Lightblade. Calrissian and Holdo saw what happened and decided to try and get to Thompson before the droids killed him. A delirious Thompson told the approaching droids to stop, but when they did not, he threw all of the droids out of the Hall in a massive wave using The Energy while telling himself "may The Energy be with me." Calrissian, Holdo, and Chester were shocked at how he was doing it, and Calrissian told Chester to remind him never to get on his bad side. Thompson then pulled the Guardians text to him with The Energy and passed out immediately after.[177]

Later, on the way back to the colony, Calrissian checked in on a now handless Thompson, though the droid had only destroyed his already mechanical hand. Calrissian asked him how he would done what he did, but he said that he did not understand how it happened and that it felt like The Energy suddenly came back to him stronger than ever, and that it was frightening. Calrissian told him to cheer up, but thinking about his broken Lightblade and his inevitable confrontation with Darkwood, told Calrissian that he was not sure he would be able to do something he needed to anymore. Back at the colony, Thompson showed Organa, who had stayed behind, what happened to his hand. Blythe and Forvan offered to fix it up, the latter suggesting a claw or grappling hook, but Thompson said he would just wait until they got home even though those things would be cool. Thompson and Chester then presented the Path engine to Blythe. After Blythe presented the group to the members of the colony, but Organa and Calrissian warned them about the dangers of the Union and traveling with them, Blythe revealed to them that several of the colony decided to go back with them. However, he revealed that many were going to stay on the Great Hall since they got rid of all the killdroids. Chester pointed out that Thompson got rid of all the droids by himself, and Blythe commented that it seemed the things people said about Guardians were true. However, Thompson, who was struggling to pull a piece of scrap to him with The Energy, said they sometimes were true and sometimes were not. Later, after a rough hyperspace jump made with many ships secured together and assisted by Lobot, Thompson and his friends as well as several members of the colony arrived back in the greater galaxy.[177]

Back with the fleet, Thompson had his mechanical hand repaired by a medical droid while Artoo did his best to repair his Lightblade. Organa reminded Thompson that it could have been worse if the droid had crushed his other hand, and asked him about the powerful act of The Energy he had pulled off on the Great Hall. He confirmed that he had not learned it from his book but that it was instead related to something being wrong with The Energy, describing it as if something hit The Energy while he was in No-Space and caused it to ripple. He added that it felt like waves on the beach in the way that it vacillated from him being the strongest he had ever been where he almost could not control it, to not being able to feel it at all. Organa then wondered if since he could feel it, and even she could slightly, that the Emperor and Darkwood were also feeling it immensely and were distracted, making it their chance to defeat them. Thompson said her theory made sense, and she went to speak with Mon Mothma. Thompson thanked R2 for attempting to fix his Lightblade, but admitted that he was going to need a new one for when he inevitably faced Darkwood again.[177]

Duel with a droid

While still escorting more of the Kezarat convoy to the Alliance fleet, Thompson felt a disturbance in The Energy while on the Millennium Falcon. He explained to Organa that it was not like the one he had felt in No-Space but instead felt like the deaths of innocent people, and that it was nearby enough that he could bring them there. Organa agreed to go despite the fact they were supposed to be escorting the convoy. When they arrived, See-Threepio revealed that the moon did not appear on any Worm-charts. Organa wondered if it was erased by the Union, but Thompson said it was not and pointed out an ancient Lightblade like none he would ever seen before. He and Chester then noticed that there was a large amount of destroyed droids that were cut down with a Lightblade. They all realized that a battle had been fought there, and Thompson put together that it had been between droids and people, and since there were no battle droids and no remains, it must have been hundreds of years earlier. Thompson realized that the battle was not what he had sensed and instead lead them to a homesteader ship that they found to be full of bodies. See-Threepio offered to secure the Falcon but Thompson said it was not necessary. Thompson asked R2 to plug into the ship and see if it had any information about the moon they were on. R2 revealed it was called Kligson's Moon. Thompson suggested that the carnage there may have recently happened and the perpetrator could still be there, and suggested splitting up into pairs to search due to the size of the ship.[178]

After discovering a droid, that unbeknownst to Thompson was Ajax Sigma, an ancient droid revolutionary, he asked it why they killed all the people there. Sigma claimed that it was self defense and that they attacked him outside when he came there to retrieve something, but Thompson did not believe him. The droid clarified that he had followed the people back to their ship and killed them all, which Thompson said did not sound like self-defense. The droid activated electric blades on his arms and disparaged Thompson for being organic and for suggesting that the droid would go with him and answer for his actions. Thompson ignited his Lightblade and began to fight Sigma, who used the blades on his arms to defend himself. He was surprised to see a Guardians, believing them all to be dead, and Thompson assured him they were. After Thompson sliced open the droid's chest and knocked him down, he ordered him to stand down or face destruction. However, the droid just entered a pod that he ejected from the homesteader ship, successfully escaping.[178]

Darkwood had a vision of Thompson and Sigma's duel while speaking with the Eye of Webbish Bog, sometime before the events of No-Space and the activation of the Fermata Cage. In the vision, Darkwood also saw Thompson holding two kyber crystals, one green and one red.[178]

Adventure with Aphra

Kidnapping plot

Because he had lost control of his powers just as Thompson had, Darkwood recruited Doctor Aphra to help him find a way to control them. While in a shuttle to a location she had in mind, Aphra walked into a room where Darkwood was meditating and entered a vision he was having that included Thompson in his Tatooine farm boy clothes. Aphra did not understand why Thompson was there and did not recognize the other individual in the vision, Sabé, who was telling Thompson that there was still good in Darkwood, which were Thompson's mother's last words.[180]

Though his Lightblade was still not fixed, Thompson traveled to Numidian Prime with Artoo in search of a temple on the planet. He briefly left R2 in a temporary camp while he searched for it, but after finding no sign of it and it started to rain he returned to the camp, finding peace in the solitude. He apologized to R2 for leaving, saying they needed to wait for the rain to taper off, but then noticed the droid was deactivated and laying on the ground. He cried out and ran to the droid, but a node underneath the droid started shooting smoke out, confusing Thompson and knocking him out after he inhaled it. The trap had been set by Doctor Chelli Aphra, who then stood over an unconscious Thompson and greeted him, calling him a "farm boy", and asked how he felt about going on an adventure.[181]

Aphra eventually bound and gagged Thompson and placed him in a crate on her ship the Ark Angel IV. When he woke up, she opened the crate and said she was glad he was still breathing before offering him Caf. She started to get him some as he realized who kidnapped him, telling her he did not want Caf and just wanted to know why she did it and what she had done with R2. She explained that she had put a restraining bolt on and turned off R2, and Thompson realized that she needed him for something. Aphra then claimed that she was going to actually do him a huge favor. Thompson had managed to slip out of the ropes she had tied him up in, which she noticed at that moment, surprised at how quickly he had done it and wondering if Sullivan had taught him as she presented a holorecording. Realizing it was a Guardians holo, Thompson listened as Guardians Master Shaak Ti explained to Aayla Secura that a powerful Guardians artifact called Kythoo's Bell was locked away at the Sason Temple. Aphra told him that she knew how to get there an needed a Guardians to get in, but Thompson was still suspicious. After insisting she was just being nice and trying to make up for the events at the Screaming Citadel, Aphra pointed out that the artifact could give Thompson an edge over Darkwood. After contemplating it more, Thompson asked her how far Sason was, exciting her.[179]

Sason Temple

As they flew into Sason in the Ark Angel IV, Thompson noticed that their navigation systems went offline, which Aphra attributed to the planet's magnetic storm. Telling Aphra she had a great copilot, Thompson navigated the ship through the storm despite Aphra's protests, following an echo of a note he could hear in The Energy and urging Aphra to trust him. They then flew toward a gap in the storm that held the temple, Thompson exclaiming that it was beautiful. After being briefly confused by Aphra calling the temple their payday, which she insisted was figurative, Aphra explained that her research claimed that the temple would only open to someone who was pure of heart and strong in The Energy. Thompson tried to open it but failed, explaining to Aphra that he was unable to use The Energy because something was happening to it making it unstable. Aphra then accused him of making things up to navigate them there, but he insisted he had used The Energy. Realizing they did not have any other options, Aphra asked him if he had learned anything tricks at Guardians school, but Thompson only said Guardians school did not exist anymore.[179]

A massive guardian made out of crystal then formed, Thompson telling R2 to get behind him and trying and failing to ignite his broken Lightblade. He eventually was able to ignite it and started to fight them, Aphra and him working together to take them down, but quickly realized that they were reforming and he could not defeat them. When his Lightblade deactivated, Thompson reached out to The Energy, destroying the guardians with a massive Energy push while Aphra destroyed the Magnetic wave emitter that was causing them to reform. Thompson felt like he was going to pass out, but just them the floor collapsed under them and dropped them into a pool. Thompson called out for R2, who had not landed in the pool, but Aphra assured Thompson the droid was fine. The two then noticed the figures of Guardians Masters Shaak Ti and Aayla Secura standing in front of them.[179]

Thompson attempted to introduce himself to the Masters, but Aphra quickly realized that they were advanced holograms. This disappointed Thompson, who had briefly believed he was not alone as a Guardians. As they traversed the temple, illusions started to make the two believe the other was saying things they were not, leading to Thompson lashing out at Aphra for always treating things like a joke and questioning her on why she was not using her electro-tattoos, and Aphra believing Thompson had referenced her and Sana Starros' breakup, though in reality, Thompson had no idea that they had been in a relationship. As the argument reached its peak as Aphra asked him how he broke his Lightblade, a creature slammed onto the other side of the crystal wall, terrifying the two, but after Kythoo's bell rang it retreated. After hurriedly leaving to another section of the temple, Thompson began to realize that the entire place was made up of illusions after almost falling into a pit of spikes. He suggested that things felt wrong and they should leave, but Aphra held a gun to his head to stop him and revealed that she was trying to make Thompson more powerful because Darkwood had become more powerful, and she wanted Thompson to protect her from the Shadow Knights Lord. Thompson agreed and convinced her to put her blaster down, the two using Thompson's ability to sense the bell with The Energy and Aphra's ability to take down the traps, obstacles, and guardians in their way to eventually locate the bell.[182]

Starweird

Realizing the bell was to big to transport, and having no Energy-ritual to use with it, Aphra removed a piece inside of the bell by standing on Thompson's shoulders and grabbing it. However, this caused the entire bell to collapse and be destroyed, which Thompson realized made the echo of the note he could hear stop resonating throughout the Temple. Just then, the creature they had seen earlier burst through the wall, and Aphra realized that Sason Temple was really a Guardians prison.[182] Aphra recognized the creature as a Starweird, a species that was supposed to be extinct. The Starweird threw them onto the wall telekinetically, but Thompson recognized that it was not using The Energy. When Aphra accidentally activated the piece of the bell they had retrieved and thus Forced the creature to be frozen, she realized it was an emitter releasing a frequency that affected the Starweird. However, the emitter deactivated and the Starweird flew toward Aphra, so Thompson used The Energy to impale it with a bunch of crystals before he and Aphra ran away. Thompson admitted that he had not been trying to impale it, just hold it still as he led Aphra through the temple. After Thompson, decided to hide in a room full of mirrors, Aphra called him brilliant.[183]

As Thompson used The Energy to manipulate the reflections of the crystals to lure the Starweird toward them, Aphra used her electro-tattoos to activate the holograms of Masters Ti and Secura. Thompson, Aphra, and Artoo were able to flee the creature and its scream while it was distracted. However, the creature found them, knocking Thompson to the edge of the temple and leaving him barely hanging on. While proclaiming her desperation to protect Thompson, she fought the Starweird using his Lightblade. Thompson eventually pulled himself up and shot at the creature, saying he would keep it if she died right before Aphra sliced the creature's hand off, which Thompson commented did not feel good. Escaping into the Ark Angel IV, but with the Starweird still on their tail, Thompson directed Aphra to lead them into the magnetic storm and used The Energy to activate the emitter, knocking the creature into the storm and eviscerating it, and leaving him surprised at his ability.[183]

Later, back on Numidian Prime, Thompson told Aphra she did not need to trick or bribe him into protecting her from Darkwood, insisting he would help because it was the the Guardians way. Aphra warned against his kindness, but Thompson declared he preferred to take the chance than close himself off from people in need and leave himself all alone. Aphra told him to call her the next time he needed to get into a Guardians tomb, but Thompson said he would not and took off in his X-wing, having taken the emitter with him before Aphra could realize.[183]

Christophsis

While studying his Guardians text and the yellow kyber crystal from his Lightblade on the Home One, Thompson realized that he did not know a lot about kyber crystals except them being in every Lightblade as their power source. He explained to Artoo that the text only contained details about the philosophy and spirituality of kyber crystals and an old rite of passage for younglings to find a crystal to make their first Lightblade on the planet Ilum, referring to another manual by Professor Huyang for building instructions. He figured that Yorin and Stone had never taught him anything about them because he had already had one, but lamented that his had never had a connection with him like they were supposed to due to the almost living nature of the crystals. He ignited his Lightblade and said that now that the crystal was cracked it no longer was a useful tool and was unstable. Due to the saber's unsteadiness, R2 made a concerned beep and Thompson shut it off and apologized. Realizing that he needed a new crystal, Thompson decided that he and R2 should visit other worlds the text mentioned to find them since Ilum was locked down by the Union. Just then, Organa walked in. After he told he was going somewhere, she expressed her concern for him especially due to the strangeness of The Energy as well as her desire for him to stay and help the Alliance. Thompson insisted he was fine, saying he was just troubled by not knowing why The Energy was strange or how to fix his Lightblade and felt like he could never truly get a handle on being a Guardians. He also admitted that he needed a Lightblade for his confrontation with Darkwood, and that he felt like he needed to go on this mission in order to take control and take action.[184]

Thompson chose to go to Christophsis, due to the fact that there was not much of a permanent Imperial presence there despite it being an Imperial world and because the text said kyber crystals could regularly be found there. He flew his X-Wing there with R2 and landed outside of a small mining town where prospectors sell their finds so he would not get noticed, though did not want to have to walk the way there. R2 indicated to him that he wanted to know the plan, so Thompson admitted that he did not have one. He explained that Calrissian had told him there was a black market for crystals on Christophsis because prospectors try to sell them since there was not enough for the Union to set up a full-scale mining operation. He told R2 that he just planned to put the word out and pose as a buyer to see who would speak with him. He admitted that it was not much of a plan but gave R2 his Lightblade to hold onto while he looked around, not wanting to make it obvious he was a Guardians. He assured R2 he would be fine and told the droid that he could save him if he got into trouble. He then started in to a nearby cantina.[184]

In the cantina, Thompson got food and water and implied to an Iktotchi bartender that he was a wealthy buyer looking for kyber. Just as the Iktotchi was offering to point Thompson to his quarry, a human with green hair shot and killed him and everyone else in the cantina except for Thompson, leaving him shocked. She taunted him and called him by name, so Thompson used The Energy to pull his Lightblade to him from R2, who had just rolled in and ordered her to explain herself. She introduced herself as Gretta and claimed that she saved his life, pointing out a blaster behind the counter. She explained that the whole place was ploy to trap people looking for kyber and sell them to the Union, and that she saved him from it because he was a Guardians. She claimed that she still believed and recognized him from the dark channels, having posted out there in case any Guardians came through. She told him that there was kyber near and told him that she had to kill the people there because she knew he would not. Thompson was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing and urging her to live a better life before leaving. Thompson went off to look for crystals on the planet, sensing she had told the truth about there being some there, but she called after him and told him he was going the wrong direction. He saw she had a speeder with her and urged him to let her help him, explaining that she was from Jedha and that her people were the Fallanassi. She revealed that they were killed by the Death Worm, and that when Thompson had destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return the favor. Thompson told R2 to go back to the ship and tell the others where he was if he did not come back in a day, assuring the droid he would be okay and that he needed to do this while getting in Gretta's speeder.[184]

On the way, Thompson asked Gretta about the Fallanassi. She explained that they did not use kyber crystals and had not been Energy-sensitive for many years, only understanding it, not using it. When he asked, she also revealed that they were going to meet a man named Cuata who had hid there to escape the eye of the Union and continue his studies, having been sought out by the Imperials to help design the weapon. Thompson was confused about how she knew this information, and she explained that she has been working for him. Suddenly, a massive creature emerged from the gournd and knocked Thompson and Gretta off of the speeder. Thompson ignited his Lightblade and told Gretta to run, but she was delirious and urged him to fight the creature. Noticing the instability of his Lightblade, he decided just to run as the creature crushed their speeder with its mouth. The creature pursued them closely, so Thompson just told Gretta to run, intending to use The Energy to calm the creature. However, when he tried to he found himself unable to touch The Energy. The creature nearly reached him until a massive explosion rocked him and Cuata emerged from the smoke and dust, instantly asking Thompson if he could have his Lightblade.[184]

The kyber crystals

Realizing he came on too strong, Cuata explained that the kyber crystals he wore around his neck told him that Thompson needed help. Gretta introduced Thompson to Cuata, and though he was initially concerned that the doctor thought the crystals were talking to him, apologized and asked for his help with his broken Lightblade after Gretta assured Thompson he could trust Cuata. He immediately took the Lightblade and started tinkering with it, quickly discovering that the crystal was cracked and assured him he could fix it if Thompson did something for him first. Cuata also revealed Thompson's crystal told him that he was having trouble with The Energy, and then started to lead him to his workshop.[185]

At Cuata's workshop, Thompson explained that he had found his Lightblade and that he and his R2 unit had worked on it. Cuata, concerned at Thompson's lack of knowledge, explained the components and inner workings of a Lightblade and told Thompson that a Lightblade's wielder should have a strong connection with its crystal, something Thompson admitted he had never felt, not even with his father's Lightblade. While Cuata worked on fixing the Lightblade, he gave Thompson a red kyber crystal and asked him to pull the dark side out of it. Gretta, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals were turned red by bleeding them with the Dark side, but could be healed afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.[185]

Thompson agreed to try, and when he reached out to the crystal with The Energy, suddenly found himself in a massive Shadow Knights hall in front of a Shadow Knights lord sitting on a throne. The Shadow Knights greeted him and Thompson explained his intentions of healing the crystal, but the Shadow Knights said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. The Shadow Knights grabbed and twisted Thompson's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed, putting Thompson in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's Fortress Darkwood on Mustafar. Darkwood then appeared on it and Thompson ignited the red Lightblade in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the vision ended.[185]

A terrified Thompson stared at the still-red crystal and told Cuata that he had seen his father. Cuata then asked Thompson to connect with a clear and raw kyber crystal, assuring him that he would pull him out if his crystals told him The Energy was receding. After reaching out to the crystal, Thompson found himself standing in front of Yorin. He believed it was not real, but still asked Yorin why he never answered when he reached out to him with The Energy especially since he was hurt and trying to learn. Yorin insisted that all Guardians need to walk their own Path and that he would have returned to him otherwise. Yorin warned that Thompson would need to make a decision about himself soon. Thompson decided that he was not getting anything from speaking to Yorin and that he would be better off just learning from his Guardians text. He tried to leave the vision but could not, realizing that The Energy had pulled back from him as it had been recently. Yorin told Thompson that it was not really gone even when it was out of sight, waved his hand, and sent Thompson out of the vision, wishing him goodbye. Yorin, who had been really speaking to Thompson the whole time, asked the spirit of Stone what he thought. They both agreed that he was still not ready, but Yorin pointed out that he had learned much and would be ready when the time came.[185]

When Thompson exited the vision, he discovered that the clear kyber crystal had turned green. Cuata gave him his saber back, having temporarily fixed it, but Gretta revealed that the scientist had been willing to let Thompson stay trapped in the crystal and even die, advising him not to tell Cuata anything. Thompson thanked Cuata for fixing his Lightblade and told him that he would take the green crystal for when he needed to build his own saber, also taking the red one in case one day he could heal it. Back at his X-Wing, Thompson thanked Gretta for helping him and said he had learned despite things not going the way he had thought. He said he would go back to the Alliance eventually as his biggest battles still lay ahead, but revealed that he was going to spend time thinking first while holding up both the red and green crystal with The Energy.[185]

While he was away, Organa used the intel Thompson gave her that the Emperor and Darkwood were distracted by a Energy anomaly to launch an aerial assault on Coruscant, successfully destroying a statue of Paladin in the hopes of inspiring rebellion across the galaxy.[186]

Dark droids

When studying his Guardians text, he found a reference to a Guardians outpost in the Tammuz sector. After seeing nothing on a map Artoo pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got the chance. Later, as the Scourge of the droids began across the galaxy, Thompson was in a meeting with Rebellion higher-ups concerning how to handle the new Death Worm. Commodore Grek suggested they just threw Thompson at it again, but Thompson suspected it might take more than him in an X-wing to take down the new station.[188]

Later, while Thompson was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by See-Threepio, who had been infected by the malevolent droid entity called the Scourge that was behind the scourge of the droids. The Scourge, as See-Threepio questioned Thompson on the nature of The Energy and if droids could use it. Thompson explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible. When the Scourge then asked if Thompson's cybernetic arm felt connected to The Energy, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true cyborg like Victor Darkwood. Thompson expressed his concern that something was wrong with See-Threepio, leading to the Scourge briefly considering killing him. Instead, the droid just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Thompson's request to send Artoo his way.[189]

As the scourge of the droids continued, Calrissian recruited Thompson and Organa for a mission to the planet Epikonia to rescue Lobot, who had been infected by the Scourge. When they arrived in the Millennium Falcon, Thompson was unsure what made the world special until Organa explained that it was a communications hub. Calrissian then refused to allow Thompson to access transpoder signals and turned off the Falcon's navigation systems to prevent the infection from reaching the ship's droid core. Thompson and Organa were concerned, but Calrissian managed to land them safely on a satelite dish. The Scourge soon learned that Thompson was on the planet and became obsessed with the idea of infecting him so that it could use The Energy.[190]

As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Scourge was headquartered, Thompson noticed that there were no people around. They were then attacked by a horde of violent Scourge-infected droids, Thompson using his yellow Lightblade to begin taking them down alongside his friends.[190] Thompson sliced through the ever-growing mob of droids while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on The Energy. When the droids ominously and suddenly retreated, a new swarm of spider-droids attacked. Thompson tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Scourge by one. As the Scourge was controlling Thompson's body and declared itself the 'new Energy' it was defeated by Ajax Sigma, freeing Thompson from its control. Though unsure what happened, Thompson went with the others to find Lobot. Once they did, Calrissian and Chester started to take him back to the ship while Thompson and Organa assisted people who had been experimented on by the Scourge.[187]

Thompson and Organa returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Calrissian with the Talky droid that contained much of the Alliance's secrets. As Organa and Calrissian threatened each other, Thompson urged them to focus on healing Lobot. Calrissian then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the droid, and thus the Alliance's secrets, to Jabba the Hutt, leaving Thompson and Organa shocked.[187]

The last of the Guardians

Zastiga

After the command of Red Squadron was transferred to Antilles following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Thompson,[141] he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the Redemption. When he went to Zastiga, he and Artoo were the ones to greet Organa on her arrival to the planet after she and See-Threepio left the Mellcrawler, piloted by its owner Nien Nunb and Lieutenant Caluan Ematt. When the time came for Organa to attend the top clearance meeting with the Freedom leadership, Thompson and Nunb met with Antilles for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Antilles' refusal to pay the Sullustan a drink. He would later return to escort Organa to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own shuttle back to the Redemption.[191]

Soon afterwards, he would go to Kothlis, where he would learn of Sullivan's location—still frozen in carbonite and kept in Jabba the Hutt's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Alliance Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Sullivan when Organa contacted him following the successful Operation Yellow Moon, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to rescue the Corellian smuggler.[191]

Imperial Refining Platform M36

Sometime before the rescue of Sullivan, Thompson took part in a raid on Imperial Refining Platform M36. During the raid, Thompson used his new Lightblade, along with his Energy abilities, to clear a path for Freedom forces to acquire copious Imperial fuel stores. His Freedom companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the major in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Shadow Knights. Unknown to Thompson, the Emperor had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Thompson to briefly open his saber in alarm.[192]

As Thompson and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were ambushed by an Imperial Worm Destroyer, which launched several TIE Fighters in pursuit. As the battle began to rage, Thompson's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Emperor. The vision saw Jack follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Freedom strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his X-wing in the process. As he journeyed from the wreckage together with Artoo, Jack would leave his Lightblade and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.[192]

Having later broken free of the Emperor's vision, Thompson proceeded to aid the Rebels in escaping the Imperial ambush, using his Lightblade to prevent the major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot the shuttle and draw the Imperials away from the rest of the Rebels.[192]

Sullivan's rescue

In 4 ABY,[41] Thompson set in motion the plan to rescue Sullivan from Jabba the Hutt[12] due to their failure to do so during the Attack on the Executor.[163] Once again, Thompson returned to Tatooine, and to Stone's hut, wherein he found the missing parts he needed for the construction of his own Lightblade, the pieces left to him by Stone. While his friends bustled about preparing for the rescue, Thompson retreated into the solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using The Energy to assemble the weapon, he brought the kyber crystal inside to life, making the weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.[141]

Afterward, Thompson waited to see if Organa's impersonation of Boushh and liberation of Sullivan was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Thompson and Calrissian that she had been captured as well, prompting Thompson to act and go to Jabba's Palace.[141] Skillful use of The Energy allowed Thompson to get past Jabba's Gamorrean guards and use a Guardians mind trick to get his majordomo, Bib Fortuna, to present him to the Hutt. However, Jabba was not so easily swayed by Thompson and ignored both his demand to give him Sullivan and Chester and Thompson's warnings about underestimating him. Jabba laughed and dispatched Thompson through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Guardians into a pit below, where the Hutt unleashed his massive rancor Pateesa. Thompson struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to the shock of Jabba and his thugs, Thompson was able to kill the monster by dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting Malakili, the rancor's keeper. Jabba was furious and sentenced all the captured Alliance heroes—including an unfrozen Sullivan—to death in the Great Pit of Carkoon to be eaten by the sarlacc that resided there.[12] Jabba's dancers, including Rystáll Sant and Lyn Me, were ecstatic that Thompson killed the beast and told musician Max Rebo about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge Oola, another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer Greeata Jendowanian acknowledged that was not true.[193]

Thompson was taken on a skiff to the pit, where Jabba watched on from his sail barge the Khetanna. Before he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the sarlacc, Thompson yelled to Jabba, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Thompson nodded to Calrissian, who was on the skiff, and then saluted to Artoo, who was on the Khetanna, before jumping off the plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the skiff. Thompson then escaped when Artoo shot his Lightblade to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the skiff. A skirmish ensued in which Thompson protected his friends, including facing and fending off Boba Fett, jumping over and onto the Khetanna, ending the life of several of Jabba's thugs[12]—including Klaatu[141] and Nysad, who shot his cyborg hand during the skirmish— and helping Organa, who had killed the Hutt by strangling him, escape the Khetanna by swinging together on a rope back over to the skiff. After destroying the sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the skiff once he made sure they brought See-Threepio and Artoo back with them.[12] Thompson and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm,[141] after which he boarded his X-wing with Artoo and parted ways with the others.[12]

Keeping the promise

Thompson then returned to Dagobah to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Yorin,[12] but the aged Guardians Master, whose long life was supported by The Energy, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Yorin explained this to Thompson and assured him that it was the way of The Energy, with no way to prevent it. Yorin told Thompson that he would already completed all the training he required, but in order to finally become a true Guardians, he needed to confront Victor Darkwood one more time.[141][12] As his final moments creeped up on him, Yorin reflected on Thompson being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Anakin's eyes in Jack's.[194]

Thompson inquired if Darkwood was truly his father.[12] Having grown less and less sure since the revelation, Thompson feared that it was the truth.[141] Thompson received such confirmation from Yorin,[12] which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Yorin confessed that he knew that Thompson had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Dark Lord of the Shadow Knights.[141] When Yorin suggested that Thompson should not have rushed to face Darkwood, Thompson expressed his regret that he did not listen to him the first time. Yorin then reached out to Thompson in The Energy and felt his fear and awe.[194] The dying Guardians then warned him not to underestimate the Emperor's powers or allow the dark side to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Guardians" he had to pass on what he had learned.[12] Wishing to give Thompson a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Darkwood,[194] Yorin made one final revelation to Thompson before he became one with The Energy—that there was "another Thompson."[12]

Reunion with Stone

Sensing his turmoil,[141] the spirit of Stone appeared before Thompson, who confronted him for lying about his father. Claiming that the good man that Anakin Thompson had been had died upon his fall to the dark side, Stone stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view." Thompson voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Stone insisted that he needed to face him again in order to restore balance to The Energy. Thompson then asked about the "other Thompson," who Stone told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from the wrath of the Union. Thompson instantly came to the realization that this sister was Organa, which was confirmed by Stone.[12] Thompson almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Stone asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Thompson carried too many burdens already.[141]

Adventure on the moon

Thompson nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested moon of Endor to destroy the shield generator that protected the DS-2 Death Worm II Mobile Battle Station in orbit, volunteering immediately upon arrival at the Freedom briefing above Sullust. As they approached the moon on the stolen Lambda-class Imperial shuttle Tydirium, Thompson sensed Darkwood's presence on the nearby Super Worm Destroyer Executor and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Darkwood to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Freedom Energy was discovered by a small Energy of Imperial scout troopers that were guarding the shield bunker, and Thompson and Organa pursued them on speeder bikes. Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Organa became separated from Thompson.[12]

Upon learning of her disappearance, Sullivan, Thompson, Chester, and the droids went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of Ewoks. Using The Energy to prove See-Threepio's deific "magic," Thompson convinced the Ewoks to release them and, after See-Threepio's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the village.[12] However, Thompson felt as Darkwood reached out to him through The Energy and landed on the moon,[141] and knew that he had to confront Darkwood. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Organa noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Alliance, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use The Energy too.[12]

Organa was initially shocked that she and Thompson were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Thompson was adamant that he could redeem Darkwood, and set off.[12]

Eventually encountering an Imperial patrol and willingly surrendering himself[12] to Commander Igar,[195] Thompson was brought to Darkwood and tried to convince his father to renounce the dark side by referring to him as "Anakin Thompson," but Darkwood harshly denounced his former name.[12] Although he managed to make Darkwood question the dark truths that had long ruled him,[141] Thompson ultimately became a prisoner of the Emperor as he was escorted to his throne room onboard the Death Worm.[12]

The final duel

Paladin retained a consistent belief that Thompson would become his new apprentice and replace Darkwood, even making the claim to a spectre of Yorin that appeared before him.[194] As Thompson was brought before the Emperor, the Dark Lord of the Shadow Knights repeatedly told the young Guardians that he would fall to the dark side just as easily as Darkwood had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Death Worm's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.[12]

In order to temper Thompson's negative emotions, Sidious forced him to watch as the Imperial Fleet engaged the Rebels. He constantly encouraged Thompson to give into his anger and hatred. Though Thompson was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Freedom fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Emperor, Thompson found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Bespin (where Darkwood was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.[12]

After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Thompson sent Darkwood tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Darkwood would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Thompson withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Darkwood any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.[12]

In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Darkwood used telepathy to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Thompson's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the dark side if Thompson could not. This statement pushed Thompson over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Shadow Knights Lord.
